  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">For Collectors</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">The following are likely to be the most sought after cards from the set by collectors—and by players who like to use higher rarity cards in their decks.</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Gallery</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">The set description for <em>Brilliant Stars </em>mentions that it contains “a special Trainer Gallery subset of 30 cards with artwork featuring fan-favorite Trainers and Pokémon.” With this and the very high count of Full Art rarity and higher cards from the Japanese set <em>VMAX Climax </em>in mind, <strong>not all of the special cards from VMAX Climax will appear in <em>Brilliant Stars</em></strong>. The cards that did not make it into Brilliant Stars’ Trainer Gallery will likely appear in future sets as part of their Trainer Gallery. To see cards that may be included in future Trainer Galleries, see the <a href="/visual/tg">Trainer Galleries article</a>.</p>
</div>
